### TB6876-N001 — Two-column loop retains two complete rows

Operation: `cif_category_cardinality`. Pillar: THOS Body. Bounded practice: synthetic crystallographic table editor. Outcome: `completed`.

Hypothesis: Validate a flat CIF loop's rectangular cardinality, case-insensitive data names, required columns, and declared row ceiling without padding absent cells. Case: Two-column loop retains two complete rows.

Frozen input:
```json
{
  "max_rows": 100,
  "required": [
    "_r_h"
  ],
  "tags": [
    "_r_h",
    "_r_k"
  ],
  "values": [
    "1",
    "2",
    "3",
    "4"
  ]
}
```

Complete matched output:
```json

  "decision": "BOUNDED_VIEW",
  "details": {
    "rows": [
      [
        "1",
        "2"
      ],
      [
        "3",
        "4"
      ]
    ],
    "tags": [
      "_r_h",
      "_r_k"
    ]
  },
  "external_credit": false,
  "reason": null,
  "source_preserved": true
}
```
